*Weiheng Zhong,Jing Bi,Victor Oancea,Hadi Meidani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PGD-NO（预计算几何分解神经算子），通过将几何编码的计算开销转移到确定性预计算阶段，并利用迭代几何分解算法提取几何令牌，实现线性内存扩展性，能够处理超过1000万节点的网格，克服了现有神经PDE求解器的单节点内存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经PDE求解器存在高内存消耗和单节点瓶颈问题，最大可处理网格分辨率受限于单个计算单元的VRAM，限制了大规模高保真工业仿真的应用。

Method: 提出PGD-NO架构，包含预计算几何分解（将几何编码开销转移到确定性预计算阶段）和迭代几何分解算法（提取几何令牌，将特征提取与解查询解耦），实现线性内存可扩展性。

Result: 在超过1000万节点的网格上实现高保真学习（现有架构通常内存耗尽），在多种工业基准测试中表现出竞争力的预测精度，并通过注意力机制提供内在可解释性。

Conclusion: PGD-NO有效克服了传统网格大小限制，为下一代大规模高保真工业设计应用提供了鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: While neural PDE solvers have demonstrated significant potential for accelerating engineering simulations, existing architectures remain constrained by high memory consumption and the single node bottleneck, where the maximum processable mesh resolution is strictly limited by the VRAM of a single compute unit. To address these challenges, we propose PGD-NO, a neural operator with Precomputed Geometry Decomposition, that relocates the computational overhead of geometric encoding to a deterministic pre-computation phase. By utilizing an iterative geometry decomposition algorithm to extract geometry tokens, our model decouples feature extraction from solution querying. This architecture enables linear memory scalability, allowing high fidelity learning on meshes exceeding 10 million nodes, a scale where existing architectures typically encounter memory exhaustion. PGD-NO demonstrates competitive predictive accuracy across diverse industrial benchmarks and provides intrinsic interpretability through attention mechanisms. By effectively overcoming traditional mesh-size constraints, PGD-NO offers a robust and efficient solution for the next generation of large-scale, high-fidelity industrial design applications.

</details>


### [182] [Rethinking Small VLM Quantization: From Component-Wise Analysis to Hardware-Aware Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08029)
*Hyeju Shin,Chorwon Kim,Ryangsoo Kim,Hark Yoo,Jaein Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Jetson Orin NX和AGX平台上系统评估了视觉语言模型（<3B参数）的组件级量化（视觉编码器、投影器、LLM骨干），通过5个假设和6种量化配置的实验，揭示了量化敏感性由结构范式（MoE vs.密集）主导而非仅规模、SigLIP编码器在Jetson Ampere上的延迟是部署特定交互而非算法缺陷、INT4量化虽降低显存但带来反量化开销降低生成速度、复合量化误差基本可加但模态对齐路径例外、能效比因内存带宽约束而跨平台显著变化等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低参数量视觉语言模型（<3B参数）的兴起推动了端侧多模态智能的应用，但组件级量化的细节理解不足成为最优部署的瓶颈，亟需系统性评估框架来指导量化策略选择。

Method: 在Jetson Orin NX和AGX平台上，将视觉编码器、投影器和LLM骨干分离，针对5个假设设计6种量化配置（涵盖不同精度组合），进行系统性对比实验，量化分析不同组件的量化敏感性、延迟、显存消耗和能效表现。

Result: (1)量化敏感性由结构范式（MoE vs.密集）主导而非仅模型规模；(2)SigLIP编码器在Jetson Ampere上产生不成比例的INT8延迟，属于部署特定的编码器-内核-硬件交互；(3)INT4量化LLM大幅减少显存但因反量化开销导致token生成变慢；(4)复合量化误差基本可加，但模态对齐路径例外且架构相关；(5)能效比因内存带宽约束在不同平台间显著变化。

Conclusion: 组件级量化需要根据具体架构和部署平台差异化策略，MoE骨干对INT4更具鲁棒性，INT4量化存在显存与速度的权衡，复合量化策略需关注模态对齐路径的特殊性，平台选择对能效比有决定性影响。

Abstract: The emergence of vision language models with fewer than 3 billion parameters has accelerated the implementation of on-device multimodal intelligence. However, a detailed understanding of component-wise quantization remains a bottleneck for optimal deployment. This paper presents a systematic evaluation framework for empirically validating five hypotheses across six quantization configurations on the Jetson Orin NX and AGX. By separating the vision encoder, projector, and large language model backbone yields the following results: (1) Quantization sensitivity is governed by the structural paradigm (MoE vs. dense) rather than scale alone, with MoE backbones mitigating INT4 noise where dense backbones degrade; (2) SigLIP encoders incur disproportionate INT8 latency on Jetson Ampere--a deployment-specific encoder-kernel-hardware interaction, not a SigLIP flaw; (3) Although INT4 quantization of LLMs greatly reduces VRAM consumption, it also causes slower token generation due to dequantization overhead; (4) Composite quantization errors are largely additive, except along the modality-alignment path, which is architecture-dependent; (5) The intelligence-per-joule profile varies significantly across platforms owing to memory bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [183] [What to Keep, What to Forget: A Rate--Distortion View of Memory Compaction in LLMs and Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08032)
*Ashwin Gerard Colaco,Nada Lahjouji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，大语言模型及其智能体在记忆(缓存键值、长提示、循环状态、会话历史)上消耗大量计算和内存，四个研究社区分别通过驱逐/量化KV缓存、剪枝/蒸馏提示、约束架构状态、整合智能体记忆来压缩。作者论证这些都是同一率-失真决策问题的实例，提出了统一的压缩目标和层无关下界，构建了七轴分类法，并发现了两个跨层模式：注意力幅度或最近性作为保留信号在所有层都会失败，而重复压缩从未被衡量。最后提出基准建议、参考实验和压缩感知设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四个独立研究社区(服务栈KV管理、提示工程、架构设计、智能体记忆)各自开发压缩技术但缺乏统一视角，导致方法难以跨层迁移、问题无法系统性理解。作者希望用率-失真理论统一这些看似分散的问题，建立共同分析框架。

Method: 提出单一压缩目标函数和层无关下界，构建七轴分类法(覆盖服务栈KV管理到智能体长期记忆)，在统一框架下跨层迁移机制，并通过小型参考实验验证。

Result: 发现两个跨层模式：(1)注意力幅度或最近性作为保留信号在所有层都会以相同方式失败——在查询未知且无法撤销时丢弃后续所需信息；(2)单轮长上下文压缩被仔细衡量，但智能体实际执行的重复压缩几乎从未被衡量，且没有基准同时固定所有层的预算轴。

Conclusion: 提出基准建议、小型参考实验、一组压缩感知设计原则，并映射了开放问题。

Abstract: Large language models, and the agents built on them, spend an ever-growing share of their compute and memory on remembering: caching attention keys and values, carrying long prompts, maintaining recurrent state, and storing what happened in previous turns and sessions. Because none of this memory is free, four largely separate research communities have each learned to compact it. They evict or quantize the KV cache, prune or distill prompts, bound architectural state, and consolidate agent memory. We argue that these are instances of one problem: a rate--distortion decision about what context-derived information to retain versus discard, at what fidelity, under a resource budget, so as to preserve downstream task utility. We make this lens precise with a single compaction objective and a layer-agnostic lower bound, use it to build a seven-axis taxonomy that classifies methods from across the stack uniformly, and use it to transfer mechanisms between layers that have never been connected, from serving-stack KV management to agent long-term memory. Two patterns hold across the survey. At every layer the signal that decides what to keep is attention magnitude or recency, and it fails in the same way everywhere, by discarding, before the query is known and with no way to undo it, information the query later needs. And while compression is measured carefully on single-turn long context, the repeated compaction that agents actually perform is almost never measured, and no benchmark holds one budget axis across all the layers at once. We turn both observations into a benchmark proposal, a small reference experiment, and a set of compaction-aware design principles, and we map the open problems.

</details>


### [184] [An exact information theory of generalization phase transitions in Bayesian diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08041)
*Henry Hunt,Mason Kamb,Surya Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯信息受限扩散（BIRD）模型，通过信息受限观测的贝叶斯后验推断来逆转扩散过程，并揭示了训练数据量、生成时间与信息受限程度三者之间的记忆-泛化信息论相变边界。实验表明，空间局部BIRD模型和早期训练的扩散模型均沿着该边界进行生成，从而规避维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散模型如何从有限训练集学习高维复杂分布而不陷入记忆化（过拟合）的机制，即破解扩散模型规避维度灾难的根本原因。

Method: 提出可解析处理的贝叶斯信息受限扩散（BIRD）模型：每个像素观测到关于噪声数据的受限信息，通过贝叶斯后验推断生成当前受限观测来自哪个训练样本，从而逆转扩散过程。该模型推广了现有使用空间局部信息限制的分析扩散模型，并在信息论框架下推导了记忆与泛化的相变边界条件。

Result: 1) 空间局部BIRD模型在训练早期能紧密近似不同架构（UNet、DiT）的扩散模型；2) 推导出记忆-泛化信息论相变边界：当受限噪声观测与训练数据之间的互信息超过训练样本数的对数时，模型记忆化，否则泛化；3) 实验验证了理论预测的相变位置；4) 发现生成过程接近记忆边界运行，BIRD模型和早期训练扩散模型均通过随时间增加信息限制来跟踪该边界。

Conclusion: 信息限制在生成式AI中扮演规避维度灾难的基础性角色，扩散模型通过动态调整信息受限程度来平衡记忆与泛化，从而在有限数据下学习高维分布。

Abstract: How diffusion models circumvent the curse of dimensionality to learn complex distributions over high dimensional spaces from a finite training set, instead of memorizing it, remains a fundamental mystery. To address this, we introduce analytically tractable Bayesian information restricted diffusion (BIRD) models, in which each pixel observes restricted information about noisy data. A BIRD model time-reverses diffusion by inferring which past training sample produced its current restricted observation using the Bayesian posterior. This model class generalizes existing analytical diffusion models that use spatially local information restriction. We show that spatially local BIRD models closely approximate trained diffusion models \textit{early in training}, across different architectures such as UNets and DiTs. Under minimal assumptions on the data distribution, we identify an information-theoretic phase boundary between memorization and generalization in the joint space of amount of training data, time in the reverse generative process, and amount of information restriction: a BIRD model memorizes when the mutual information between its restricted noisy observations and the training data exceeds the log number of training points, and it generalizes otherwise. Experiments across a range of datasets confirm our theoretically predicted location for the transition. We find that generation proceeds near the edge of memorization: both spatially local BIRD models and early-training diffusion models track the memorization-generalization phase boundary by increasingly restricting information over time. Overall, our results reveal a fundamental role for information restriction in generative AI to circumvent the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [185] [Who Analyses the Analyser? Self-Validating LLM Hazard Analysis with Constitutional Meta-STPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08054)
*Samuel Tetteh,Udip Shrestha,Joshua R. Waite,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Constitutional Meta-STPA，一种对AI辅助安全分析工具自身进行STPA分析的方法，通过自推导生成包含21条工具原则和8条元安全原则的治理宪法，并发现该元分析层受限于模型能力而非宪法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辅助安全分析工具（如STPA）只分析目标系统，却从未对工具本身进行安全分析，存在幻觉、不可验证约束、缺乏审计追踪等盲点。作者试图回答"谁来分析分析者？"这一被忽视的问题。

Method: 提出Constitutional Meta-STPA方法：对AI辅助安全工具这一系统进行元STPA分析，从损失→危险→不安全控制动作(UCAs)→约束的推导链中生成治理宪法，包含21条工具原则和8条元安全原则，并通过宪法边际覆盖算子形式化度量。

Result: 前沿模型组合（claude-opus-4.8+claude-sonnet-4）能恢复18/21条规范原则和全部8条治理原则，而较弱模型仅恢复12/21和3/8，说明元层受限于模型能力而非宪法；同样的8条元安全原则也能从另一个独立工具中复现。

Conclusion: 元安全分析层是模型能力受限的，而非宪法受限的，表明该方法具有鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted to draft the artifacts of safety analysis such as, losses, hazards, Unsafe Control Actions (UCAs), and safety constraints, inside rigorous processes such as Systems-Theoretic Process Analysis (STPA). Yet a blind spot runs through this fast-growing literature: every system gets analysed except the LLM-assisted tool doing the analysing, which is itself a safety-relevant system that can hallucinate standards, emit unverifiable constraints, and leave no audit trail from prompt to artifact. We take seriously the question the field has skipped -- {who analyses the analyser?} and answer it by turning STPA on the tool itself. We present \{Constitutional Meta-STPA}, an LLM-assisted STPA tool built around a closed loop: the tool runs a {meta-STPA} of the class of AI-assisted safety tools and {derives} rather than asserts, its governance constitution from the resulting loss$\to$hazard$\to$UCA$\to$constraint chain, yielding a published constitution of $21$ Tool Principles and $8$ Meta-Safety Principles, each bound to a code enforcement point. We formalise the measured object as a constitution-marginal coverage operator over a principle set $P$ ($|P|{=}29$) with a soundness lemma that isolates coverage from model and scanner, and report four findings. {(i)~Self-derivation:} a frontier ensemble ({claude-opus-4.8}${+}${claude-sonnet-4}) recovers $18/21$ canonical and all $8/8$ governance principles from the tool's own design, while a weaker pair recovers $12/21$ and $3/8$, so the meta layer is model-limited, not constitution-limited, and the same $8/8$ re-emerge from a second, independently authored tool.

</details>


### [186] [Reinforcing the Generation Order of Multimodal Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08056)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhe Wang,Sourav Bhabesh,Dmitriy Bespalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过GRPO学习生成顺序控制模块，在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务中分别取得4.08%和4.85%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在数学推理和代码生成中已证明自适应生成顺序的有效性，但文本到图像生成和多模态理解中模型logits不足以确定最优生成顺序，需要新的方法来优化生成顺序。

Method: 引入一个可学习的控制模块，通过GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习算法训练，以确定文本到图像生成和多模态理解中的生成顺序。

Result: 在GenEval基准上实现4.08%的相对改进，在VLMEvalKit上实现4.85%的相对改进，显著提升细粒度空间关系捕捉能力和多模态推理性能。

Conclusion: 学习生成顺序控制模块能有效提升扩散语言模型在文本到图像对齐和多模态理解任务上的表现，证明了该方法的广泛有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in natural language generation tasks. Recent research demonstrates that adaptive token generation ordering can significantly improve performance in mathematical reasoning and code synthesis applications. In this work, we investigate the optimization of generation order for both text-to-image synthesis and multimodal understanding. We first establish that, unlike structured problems in language generation such as Sudoku puzzles, model logits alone are insufficient for determining optimal generation sequences in text-to-image generation and multimodal understanding. To address this challenge, we introduce a learnable control module trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to determine the generation order. Our results demonstrate that learning this control block substantially improves both text-to-image alignment and multimodal understanding in DLMs. In particular, it enhances the model's ability to capture fine-grained spatial relationships in generated images while also strengthening performance on multimodal reasoning and comprehension tasks. We evaluate our framework on GenEval, an object-focused benchmark for text-to-image alignment, where it achieves 4.08% relative improvements. In addition, experiments on VLMEvalKit confirm 4.85% relative improvements in multimodal understanding, highlighting the broad effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [187] [Towards Efficient Large Language Model Serving: A Survey on System-Aware KV Cache Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08057)
*Jiantong Jiang,Peiyu Yang,Rui Zhang,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向LLM服务的系统感知KV缓存基础设施（sKis），从系统行为视角将现有工作组织为时间（执行与调度）、空间（放置与迁移）和结构（表示与保留）三个维度，并分析了跨行为协同设计与行为-目标关联，为现代LLM服务中的KV缓存设计提供了系统化理解与创新基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务系统面临内存密集和高成本问题，KV缓存是低延迟、高吞吐推理的关键，但缺乏从系统行为角度对其基础设施的系统性梳理和未来方向分析。

Method: 从系统行为视角出发，将现有KV缓存相关工作分为三个维度：执行与调度（时间维度）、放置与迁移（空间维度）、表示与保留（结构维度），并进一步分析跨行为协同设计（co-design）以及行为与优化目标之间的关联关系。

Result: 系统化梳理了sKis领域的研究工作，揭示了不同系统行为维度之间的协同设计机会，以及行为与性能/成本目标之间的映射关系。

Conclusion: 本文为理解LLM服务基础设施中KV缓存设计提供了系统化基础，指出了未来在跨行为协同优化方面的研究方向。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancements of large language models (LLMs), LLM serving systems remain memory-intensive and costly. The key-value (KV) cache, which stores KV tensors during autoregressive decoding, is crucial for enabling low-latency, high-throughput LLM inference serving. In this survey, we focus on system-aware KV infrastructure for serving LLMs (abbreviated as sKis). We revisit recent work from a system behavior perspective, organizing existing efforts into three dimensions: execution and scheduling (temporal), placement and migration (spatial), and representation and retention (structural). Furthermore, we analyze cross-behavior co-design affinity and behavior-objective links, highlighting future opportunities. Our work systematizes a rapidly evolving area, providing a foundation for understanding and innovating KV cache designs in modern LLM serving infrastructure.

</details>


### [188] [When Thinking Hurts: Epistemic Signals in the Reasoning Chains of Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08059)
*Mayank Singal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对思考模式视觉语言模型（VLM）中答案熵行为进行了三家族实证刻画，发现Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking答案熵完全失效（AUROC=0.492），GLM-4.1V-9B-Thinking未失效（0.716），InternVL3-8B选择性思考（50%样本生成链）。在所有思考模式模型中，思考链熵比答案熵更可靠（0.647/0.759/0.608 vs 0.492/0.716/0.602）。在VQAv2和HallusionBench上也验证了链熵优势，并发现结构化弃权现象（影响12-22%查询），弃权门可将准确率从71.0%提升至93.8%（覆盖率62.7%），无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉语言模型的不确定性量化仅关注答案令牌分布，而思考模式VLM在生成答案前会产出内部思考链，其熵行为尚未被系统刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，理解思考模式VLM中答案熵与思考链熵的行为模式及其与不确定性量化的关系。

Method: 在POPE对抗样本上运行四种模型（Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking、GLM-4.1V-9B-Thinking、InternVL3-8B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B），计算答案熵和思考链熵的AUROC指标；在300样本VQAv2和HallusionBench上进行验证；分析结构化弃权现象并设计弃权门策略。

Result: 识别出三种熵行为模式：完全崩塌（Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking, AUROC=0.492）、无崩塌（GLM-4.1V-9B-Thinking, 0.716）和选择性思考（InternVL3-8B, 50%样本生成链）。思考链熵整体优于答案熵（分别0.647/0.759/0.608 vs 0.492/0.716/0.602）。VQAv2上链熵0.680优于答案熵0.595，自由回答差距最大（0.733 vs 0.467）。发现结构化弃权（12-22%查询），弃权门提升准确率从71.0%到93.8%且覆盖率62.7%。

Conclusion: 思考链熵比答案熵更适合作为思考模式VLM的不确定性指标，但存在模型间异质性。结构化弃权现象可被利用为无需额外成本的不确定性过滤策略，有效提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification for visual language models (VLMs) conventionally targets the answer token distribution. We provide the first three-family empirical characterisation of answer entropy behaviour in thinking-mode VLMs. Running four models on identical POPE adversarial samples, we find three qualitatively distinct patterns: Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking shows complete collapse (ans H AUROC = 0.492); GLM-4.1V-9B-Thinking shows no collapse (0.716); and InternVL3-8B shows selective thinking (chains on only 50% of samples, ans H = 0.675 full / 0.602 thinking-only). Across all three thinking-mode models, thinking chain entropy outperforms answer entropy on the subset where chains are generated (0.647, 0.759, 0.608 vs. 0.492, 0.716, 0.602 respectively), suggesting chain signals are the more reliable predictor whenever chains are present. This holds strongly for Qwen and GLM, but with only marginal and statistically unreliable advantage for InternVL3 (n_FP = 17). A 300-sample VQAv2 pilot confirms chain entropy (0.680) outperforms answer entropy (0.595) on VQAv2 questions, with the gap largest for free-form answers (0.733 vs. 0.467). On harder reasoning tasks (HallusionBench) both Qwen models show moderate signal (approx. 0.64), consistent with incomplete pre-commitment on difficult questions. We additionally document structured abstention affecting 12-22% of queries with asymmetry toward absent-object queries, and a practical abstention gate raising accuracy from 71.0% to 93.8% at 62.7% coverage with no additional inference cost.

</details>


### [189] [Cross-Modal Generative Framework for Signal Translation from Fetal-Maternal Electrocardiograms to Fetal Doppler Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08073)
*Tongli Su,Alireza Rafiei,Marly van Assen,Reza Sameni,Gari D. Clifford,Faezeh Marzbanrad,Nasim Katebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨模态生成框架，利用扩张卷积和跨模态注意力机制，从胎儿/母体ECG信号合成胎儿多普勒超声包络，实现50%以上的误差降低，并量化了母胎心血管耦合对胎儿血流动力学的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿心电图(fECG)和多普勒超声分别反映心脏电活动和机械血流动力学，两者互补但关系尚不明确。理解从fECG可恢复与不可恢复的多普勒分量，有助于区分电活动和机械因素对胎儿循环的相对贡献。此外，母胎心脏耦合的临床证据提示母体心血管动力学可能蕴含胎儿血流动力学信息，但缺乏有效的计算模型来量化这种关系。

Method: 提出跨模态生成框架，结合扩张卷积与跨模态注意力机制选择性融合母体ECG信息，并采用自注意力捕获长程时间依赖。使用来自39例妊娠的885段同步胎儿/母体ECG和多普勒包络数据进行训练。

Result: 合成的多普勒包络功率谱密度均方误差(PSD MSE)为49.9±15.8 dB²，比双通道基线低51%；心率误差为4.71±0.77 bpm，比基线提升1.5%。跨模态注意力相比简单双通道拼接降低39%的PSD MSE，量化了母胎耦合的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了从双导联ECG合成多普勒包络，通过分析可恢复和残差多普勒分量，能够量化ECG无法捕获的纯机械贡献，从而促进更全面的胎儿心血管功能评估。

Abstract: Fetal electrocardiogram (fECG) and Doppler ultrasound provide complementary views of fetal cardiovascular function: fECG captures electrical activity while Doppler reflects mechanical hemodynamics shaped by factors such as placental resistance and vascular compliance. Understanding the recoverable and unrecoverable Doppler components through reconstruction from fECG offers insight into the relative contributions of electrical versus mechanical factors in fetal circulation, thereby informing clinical decisions. In addition, clinical evidence of maternal-fetal cardiac coupling suggests that maternal cardiovascular dynamics may also inform fetal hemodynamics. To computationally model these relationships, we propose a cross-modal generative framework combining dilated convolutions with cross-modal attention to selectively incorporate maternal ECG and self-attention to capture long-range temporal dependencies. Trained on 885 synchronized fetal/maternal ECG and Doppler envelope segments from 39 pregnancies, our model synthesizes Doppler envelopes with power spectral density mean squared error (PSD MSE) of 49.9 +/- 15.8 dB^2 (51% lower than two-channel baseline) and heart-rate error of 4.71 +/- 0.77 bpm (1.5% better than baseline; negligible relative to the 110-160 bpm physiological range). Cross-modal attention yields a 39% PSD MSE reduction over naive dual-channel concatenation, quantifying the contribution of maternal-fetal coupling. Our proposed framework advances computational modeling of the maternal-fetal cardiovascular system by enabling the synthesis of Doppler envelopes from dual-lead ECG. By analysis of both recoverable and residual Doppler components, this approach enables quantification of the purely mechanical contributions to Doppler waveforms -- those not recoverable from electrical recordings -- ultimately facilitating a more comprehensive fetal assessment.

</details>


### [190] [Modular Pretraining Enables Access Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08077)
*Ethan Roland,Murat Cubuktepe,Erick Martinez,Stijn Servaes,Keenan Pepper,Mike Vaiana,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena,Judd Rosenblatt,Addie Foote,Cem Anil,Alex Cloud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出梯度路由辅助模块（GRAM）预训练方法，通过在神经网络中添加选择性更新的模块实现能力隔离，仅需单一模型即可模拟多模型访问控制，在多个领域实验中接近数据过滤效果且成本降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力具有双重用途（如医疗/生物武器），需要访问控制机制。然而为不同用户训练和部署多个具有不同能力的模型成本过高，亟需一种经济高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出GRAM预训练方法，在神经网络中添加辅助模块并选择性地更新这些模块以诱导能力专业化；推理时通过消融特定模块移除对应能力，近似于在过滤数据上训练的模型。

Result: 在合成故事及真实双重用途数据（病毒学、网络安全、核物理、专业代码）上，GRAM能禁用目标能力同时保留其他能力；在微调恢复能力方面优于事后遗忘；从50M到5B参数的Chinchilla最优缩放分析显示，GRAM紧密追踪数据过滤效果，且训练成本与支持的能力配置文件数量无关，在5配置文件设置下比数据过滤减少5倍成本。

Conclusion: GRAM是一种有效的、可扩展的访问控制预训练方法，能够以单一模型的成本实现多模型的能力隔离效果，并具有良好的抗微调恢复能力。

Abstract: AI developers face a dual-use dilemma. An AI capability that helps one user cure a disease can help another synthesize one. This dilemma could be resolved with access control, limiting dual-use AI capabilities to trusted deployments with a legitimate need. A gold standard for access control would be to serve separate models with different capabilities to different users. However, training and deploying multiple models is prohibitively expensive. To address this challenge, we propose gradient-routed auxiliary modules (GRAM), a pre-training method that adds modules to a neural network and selectively updates them to induce specialization. Ablating a module at inference time removes its capability from the network, approximating a model trained on filtered data. We evaluate GRAM on synthetic stories and realistic dual-use data spanning virology, cybersecurity, nuclear physics, and specialized code. These experiments show that GRAM disables targeted capabilities while preserving the rest, and resists their recovery under finetuning better than post-hoc unlearning. Most importantly, a Chinchilla-optimal scaling analysis from 50M to 5B parameters shows that the gap between data-filtered and full-data models widens with scale on removed capabilities but stays small on retained ones, and that GRAM closely tracks data filtering. GRAM's training cost is independent of the number of supported capability profiles, yielding a 5x reduction over data filtering in our 5-profile setting.

</details>


### [191] [Deep Learning Method for Stationary Distribution of Reflected Brownian Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08091)
*Jim Dai,Zhanhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的框架，通过基本伴随关系（BAR）学习高维反射布朗运动（RBM）的拉普拉斯变换，从而高效准确地计算尾概率等性能指标，在已知真实解的实例中实现了近乎完美的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射布朗运动（RBM）的平稳分布在高维随机系统分析中至关重要，但其闭式解仅存在于少数特例，尾概率等关键性能指标的计算更为困难，缺乏通用的高效求解方法。

Method: 基于基本伴随关系（BAR）设计损失函数，结合精心设计的训练数据采样流程和神经网络架构，学习高维RBM的拉普拉斯变换。

Result: 在已知真实尾概率的RBM实例上，该方法在高维设置下实现了近乎完美的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该深度学习框架展示了作为分析可解析处理范围之外的随机系统通用工具的潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: The stationary distribution of reflected Brownian motion (RBM) plays an important role in the analysis of high-dimensional stochastic systems, yet closed-form solutions are known only for a few special cases. Computing important performance metrics, such as tail probabilities, is even more intractable, despite their practical relevance. In this paper, we develop a deep learning approach that accurately and efficiently learns the Laplace transform of high-dimensional RBMs based on the basic adjoint relationship (BAR). Our framework combines a careful design of the loss function, training data sampling procedure, and neural network architecture. We evaluate the proposed method on RBM instances with known ground-truth tail probabilities and demonstrate near-perfect prediction in high-dimensional settings, highlighting its potential as a general tool for analyzing stochastic systems beyond analytically tractable regimes. Our code can be found at https://github.com/zhangz73/NN4MGF.

</details>


### [192] [Stochastic Order Learning: An Approach to Rank Estimation Using Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08103)
*Chaewon Lee,Seon-Ho Lee,Chang-Su Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机顺序学习（SOL）框架，将带噪声的排序估计重新定义为随机排序问题，通过判别损失和随机顺序损失两种互补目标学习嵌入空间，实现多种标签噪声下的可靠排序估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序标注常呈现结构化不确定性而非简单标签损坏，传统方法难以处理带噪声的序数标签。

Method: 提出随机顺序学习（SOL）框架：将排序估计重构为随机排序问题，每个实例天然对应多个合理排序；包含判别损失（结构实例-质心交互）和随机顺序损失（强制执行实例间概率排序关系）两个互补目标。

Result: 在多种数据集上，SOL在不同类型和水平的标签噪声下均能实现可靠的排序估计。

Conclusion: SOL通过建模序数标签不确定性，有效解决了带噪声环境下的排序估计难题。

Abstract: Rank estimation under label noise poses a fundamental challenge, as ordinal annotations often exhibit structured uncertainty rather than simple label corruption. In this paper, we reformulate rank estimation with noisy ordinal labels as a stochastic ordering problem, in which each instance is inherently associated with multiple plausible ranks instead of a single deterministic label. Based on this view, we propose stochastic order learning (SOL), a learning framework that captures ordinal label uncertainty and learns an embedding space through two complementary objectives: a discriminative loss that structures instance--centroid interactions and a stochastic order loss that enforces probabilistic ordering relations between instances. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that SOL enables reliable rank estimation under various types and levels of label noise. The source code is available at https://github.com/cwlee00/SOL.

</details>


### [193] [Vanilla SGD with Momentum Survives Heavy-Tailed Noise: Convergence Analysis without Gradient Clipping or Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08104)
*Ryusei Yamada,Naoki Sato,Hideaki Iiduka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了vanilla SGD及其动量变体在重尾噪声下的收敛性能，在没有梯度裁剪或归一化的情况下，给出了强凸、凸和非凸目标函数的首次全面收敛分析，证明了vanilla方法在此场景下的收敛率劣于裁剪/归一化SGD的最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多通过梯度裁剪、归一化等专门修改来处理重尾噪声，但vanilla SGD（尤其是带动量版本）在重尾噪声下的基础性能尚未被充分理解，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 理论推导vanilla SGD及带动量SGD在重尾噪声下对于强凸、凸和非凸目标的收敛界，不采用任何梯度控制机制，并通过合成函数实验验证理论结果。

Result: vanilla SGD及其动量变体在重尾噪声下的收敛率劣于裁剪/归一化SGD所能达到的最优收敛率，揭示了vanilla方法在此场景下的固有限制。

Conclusion: vanilla SGD在重尾噪声下存在本质的性能瓶颈，即使加入动量也无法弥补，需要梯度控制机制才能达到最优收敛。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is a cornerstone of modern optimization. While its performance under heavy-tailed noise is often addressed through specialized modifications such as gradient clipping or normalization, we investigate a more fundamental question: how does vanilla SGD, particularly with momentum, perform in the presence of heavy-tailed noise? In this paper, we refine existing convergence results for vanilla SGD and, more importantly, provide the first comprehensive convergence analysis of vanilla SGD with momentum for strongly convex, convex, and nonconvex objectives, without employing any gradient control mechanisms. Our results demonstrate that the obtained convergence rates are inferior to the optimal rates achieved by clipped or normalized variants of SGD, thereby revealing inherent limitations of vanilla methods under heavy-tailed noise. The theoretical findings are supported by experiments on synthetic functions.

</details>


### [194] [Contrastive Order Learning: A General Framework for Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08109)
*Chaewon Lee,BeomJun Shim,Kwang Pyo Choi,Chang-Su Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比排序学习（ConOrd），一种结合对比学习与排序学习优势的序数回归框架，通过对比排序损失（含基于排序差异的软亲和力与差异权重）实现细粒度序数关系建模，在面龄估计、盲图像/视频质量评估任务上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习虽能有效利用批次内所有样本，但忽略了标签间的固有排序关系；而排序学习虽显式建模标签序数性，但常依赖局部的间隔比较，难以捕获全局序数结构。现有方法无法同时兼顾全局对比与序数关系建模。

Method: 提出对比排序损失（contrastive order loss），引入基于排序差异的软亲和力权重（soft affinity）和差异权重（disparity weights），对所有样本对进行细粒度的序数关系建模，在对比学习框架中融入序数信息。

Result: 在面龄估计、盲图像质量评估、盲视频质量评估等多个序数回归任务上，ConOrd一致达到最先进性能，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ConOrd有效融合了对比学习与排序学习的优势，解决了各自局限性，为序数回归提供了一种通用且高效的框架。

Abstract: We propose contrastive order learning (ConOrd), a contrastive learning framework for ordinal regression that integrates the strengths of contrastive learning and order learning. While contrastive learning effectively leverages all samples in a batch, it typically ignores the inherent ordering among rank labels. Conversely, order learning explicitly models label ordinality but often relies on local, margin-based comparisons, limiting its ability to capture global ordinal structure. ConOrd addresses these limitations by introducing a contrastive order loss with soft affinity and disparity weights based on rank differences, enabling fine-grained modeling of ordinal relationships across all sample pairs within a batch. Extensive experiments on a range of ordinal regression tasks, including facial age estimation, blind image quality assessment, and blind video quality assessment, demonstrate that ConOrd consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance and generalizes well across diverse ordinal regression scenarios. The source code is available at https://github.com/cwlee00/ConOrd.

</details>


### [195] [Workload-Preserving Differentially Private Synthetic Data for Causal Inference via Maximum-Entropy Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08122)
*Amir Asiaee,Kaveh Aryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私合成数据中的因果推断问题，提出因果工作负载（causal workloads）——一种围绕双重稳健因果估计的正交矩设计的DP查询集，并通过Causal-AIM自适应选择器与噪声感知多重插补（NA+MI）方法，实现了在严格隐私预算下对ATE、ATT等因果效应的高效估计，同时揭示了分布保真度与因果推断有效性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于工作负载的差分隐私合成数据方法能够实现良好的分布保真度，但通用边际查询并未专门保留因果推断所需的处理组-对照组平衡和结果矩信息，导致平均处理效应（ATE）等因果量的估计不够准确。

Method: 提出因果工作负载（causal workloads），围绕双重稳健因果估计器的正交矩设计DP查询集；引入Causal-AIM自适应工作负载选择器；提出噪声感知多重插补（NA+MI）程序用于从DP合成数据构建置信区间；将ATE误差分解为采样、隐私、工作负载逼近、蒙特卡洛和校准五项。

Result: 因果工作负载在严格隐私预算和校准不确定性方面表现最优，而通用工作负载在隐私宽松时对点RMSE更具优势；ATTE、ATT和亚组分析可在一次隐私预算内完成。

Conclusion: 分布保真度有助于点估计精度，但有效的因果推断需要保留因果矩并将DP噪声传播至推断过程，而非将合成行视为真实数据；这揭示了分布保真度与因果有效性之间的核心权衡。

Abstract: Workload-based differentially private (DP) synthetic data methods privately measure aggregate queries and post-process the noisy answers into synthetic records. Generic workloads can achieve strong distributional fidelity, but causal estimands such as the average treatment effect (ATE) depend on treatment-arm balance and outcome moments that generic marginals need not preserve. We propose causal workloads: DP query sets designed around the orthogonal moments used by doubly robust causal estimators. The released workload can be used directly by stable moment-map estimators or reconstructed by maximum-entropy calibration into reusable synthetic data; our theory decomposes ATE error into sampling, privacy, workload-approximation, Monte Carlo, and calibration terms. We also introduce Causal-AIM, an adaptive workload selector, and a noise-aware multiple-imputation (NA+MI) procedure for confidence intervals from DP synthetic data. Because the workload is released once, the same DP synthetic table can support ATE, ATT, and subgroup analyses without additional privacy spending. Empirically, causal workloads are most useful at strict privacy budgets and for calibrated uncertainty, while generic workloads often retain an advantage for point RMSE as privacy relaxes. The broader lesson is a tradeoff: distributional fidelity can help point accuracy, but valid causal inference requires preserving causal moments and propagating DP noise rather than treating synthetic rows as real.

</details>


### [196] [DeepPySR -- A Symbolic Regression Framework with Dynamic Pruning, Pareto Selection, and Hierarchical Composition for Real-World Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08150)
*Fuling Chen,Kevin Vinsen,Phillip Melton,Rae-Chi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepPySR通过动态变量剪枝、指数帕累托选择和多层层次化符号组合，解决了符号回归在高维输入、帕累托前沿选择和数据结构不规则性三方面的挑战，在多个基准上优于PySR等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归能产生玻璃箱模型（可直接解释的公式），对临床医学和社会科学至关重要，但现有SR方法面临高维输入、帕累托前沿公式的原则性选择、以及多重共线性和类别不平衡等数据不规则性三大挑战。

Method: 提出DeepPySR，包含：1）动态变量剪枝调度以在搜索过程中移除无关特征；2）指数帕累托选择准则，消除准确性与复杂度之间的权衡；3）用于层次化符号组合的多层架构。

Result: 在四个费曼物理基准和七个生物医学/社会科学数据集上，DeepPySR在体脂率（R²: 0.794 vs 0.702）、心脏病（F1: 0.898 vs 0.787）、学生成绩（R²: 0.964 vs 0.948）和Raine BMI（R²: 0.525 vs 0.370）上均优于PySR等基线。

Conclusion: DeepPySR在多个领域产生可解释的公式，结果与领域风险因素一致，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) discovers analytical equations from data, yielding glass-box models with directly interpretable formulas, unlike black-box methods that rely on unstable post-hoc tools such as SHAP or LIME. This transparency is crucial in clinical medicine and social science, but SR faces three challenges: high-dimensional inputs, principled selection of Pareto-front formulae, and data irregularities such as multicollinearity and class imbalance. We introduce DeepPySR, which addresses these issues with a dynamic variable-pruning schedule to remove irrelevant features during search, an exponential Pareto selection criterion that eliminates trade-offs between accuracy and complexity, and a multi-layer architecture for hierarchical symbolic composition. On four Feynman physics benchmarks and seven biomedical and social-science datasets, DeepPySR outperforms PySR and baselines on body fat (R$^2$: 0.794 vs.\ 0.702), heart disease (F1: 0.898 vs.\ 0.787), student performance (R$^2$: 0.964 vs.\ 0.948), and Raine BMI (R$^2$: 0.525 vs.\ 0.370), producing interpretable formulas aligned with domain risk factors.

</details>


### [197] [Understanding Layer Patching in Model Size Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08170)
*Sara Kangaslahti,Jonathan Geuter,Nihal V. Nayak,Marco Fumero,Francesco Locatello,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究零样本模型尺寸插值中的学生层选择问题，将寻找最优层子集形式化为有向无环图上的最短路径问题，实验发现从首层或末层开始的简单顺序拼接策略表现强劲，并提出基于KL散度的贪心算法KLPatch以近似求解该优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有boomerang蒸馏方法通过逐层拼接学生和教师模型的层来实现模型尺寸插值，但缺乏对学生层选择策略的系统性研究，无法保证所选层子集在性能和尺寸之间取得最优平衡。

Method: 将最优层子集选择形式化为优化问题，证明可等价为有向无环图中的最短路径问题；提出基于KL散度的贪心拼接算法KLPatch；在多个模型族上进行系统的实验比较。

Result: 简单顺序拼接策略（从头到尾或从尾到头）在实践中表现强劲；KLPatch算法通常优于从尾到头的拼接方法，能近似求解优化问题；拼接方式对插值行为的影响在不同模型族间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 提供了层拼接影响模型尺寸插值的原理性理解，为构建近最优插值模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Zero-shot model size interpolation aims to create new models of intermediate target sizes by combining existing models without additional training. Recent work on boomerang distillation [Kangaslahti et al., 2026] shows that a student language model distilled from a larger teacher can be expanded by iteratively patching its layers, replacing student layers with contiguous blocks of teacher layers to obtain models whose size and performance interpolate between the student and the teacher. In this work, we provide the first systematic study of student-layer selection for model size interpolation. We cast finding the optimal layer subset for each model size as an optimization problem and prove it can be viewed as a shortest-path problem in a certain acyclic graph. In experiments, we show that patching strongly shapes interpolation behavior, with effects that vary substantially across model families. We find that simple sequential strategies--patching either from the first layer to the last or from the last to the first--often achieve surprisingly strong performance in practice. We further introduce KLPatch, a greedy patching algorithm based on KL divergence, which often improves over last-to-first patching and approximately solves the optimization problem. Together, our results provide a principled understanding of how layer patching affects model size interpolation and offer practical guidance for constructing near-optimal interpolated models.

</details>


### [198] [Open-ended Multi-agent Autocurricula via Visual Inspection of Policies with Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08193)
*Lorenzo Pantè,Andrea Fanti,Roberto Capobianco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VIP方法，利用视频语言模型（VLM）直接分析智能体策略的录播视频来生成课程学习建议，并在SMAC多智能体任务中验证其有效性，性能优于基于文本或标量分数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式的强化学习课程学习中，评估任务相对于智能体当前学习进展的难度是一个关键挑战。现有方法依赖标量任务分数或文本摘要来评估，但这些方式可能丢失丰富的策略行为信息。作者希望通过直接检查策略行为的录播视频来更有效地评估任务难度并生成课程建议。

Method: 提出Visual Inspection of Policies (VIP)，利用轻量级开源视频语言模型VideoLLaMa2-7B直接处理智能体策略的录播视频，并基于视频内容生成课程推荐。在StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge (SMAC)环境中进行实验验证。

Result: VIP在SMAC任务中生成的课程比纯文本消融版本以及依赖标量任务分数的方法更有效。

Conclusion: 利用VLM直接分析策略视频是评估任务难度和生成课程学习的有效途径，即使使用轻量级开源模型也能取得优于文本或标量分数方法的效果。

Abstract: Open-ended curricula in Reinforcement Learning (RL) aim to train generally-capable agents by identifying tasks that facilitate learning increasingly complex skills. A major challenge when designing such curricula is assessing task difficulty relative to the agent's current learning progress. While previous work has explored using scalar task scores or textual summaries of the agent's behavior, here we study a different approach: directly inspecting policy behavior via recorded episode videos. We introduce a simple yet effective instantiation of this approach which leverages a Video Language Model (VLM) to both process these videos and provide curriculum recommendations, which we call Visual Inspection of Policies (VIP). Since videos can naturally contain any number of controllable agents, we empirically study VIP on the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge (SMAC). We show that even with a lightweight and openly accessible VLM (VideoLLaMa2-7B), VIP can use policy videos to generate more effective curricula than both its text-only ablation and methods that rely on scalar task scores.

</details>


### [199] [PIT-SUN: A Deployable Empirical Marginal Transform Framework with Expectation-Consistent Recovery for Regression in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08202)
*Mingyu Zhao,Zhaohan Li,Zhenxiong Miao,Xu Zhang,Dewei Leng,Yanan Niu,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIT-SUN提出了一种基于概率积分变换的无偏恢复框架，通过经验边际表定义有界正态分数坐标、逆分位数查找、方差控制的恢复基和漂移监控，使用乘法SUN恢复替代直接逆变换来估计原始空间条件期望，有效解决了重尾、零膨胀和多峰分布下的期望一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MSE损失在处理重尾、零膨胀和多峰目标时梯度不稳定，导致均值崩溃和尾部收缩。非线性边际变换虽能缓解尺度冲突，但直接逆变换无法保持期望一致性（仅仿射逆变换可保证，但无法同时提供有界尾部压缩）。现有条件线性恢复方法仍面临坐标选择、逆查找、恢复基和部署监控等问题。

Method: PIT-SUN框架：1) 使用经验边际表定义有界正态分数坐标；2) 构建逆分位数查找；3) 设计方差控制的恢复基；4) 实施漂移监控；5) 采用乘法SUN（Synthetic Unbiased Normal）恢复替代直接逆变换来估计原始空间期望。

Result: 在合成分布、公开基准、大规模工业数据集和在线部署上的实验表明，PIT-SUN在点估计精度、校准质量和排序质量上均有一致提升，且部署开销轻量。

Conclusion: PIT-SUN是一个可部署的经验边际恢复框架，通过避免直接逆变换而使用乘法SUN恢复，在保持期望一致性的同时实现了有效的尾部压缩，为价值驱动推荐系统中的条件期望估计提供了一种实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating original-space conditional expectations is central to value-driven recommender systems, including dwell time, GMV, and LTV forecasting. Standard MSE is expectation-consistent in principle, but its gradients become unstable on heavy-tailed, zero-inflated, and multimodal targets, causing mean collapse and tail shrinkage. Target transformation alleviates this scale conflict, yet any useful nonlinear marginal transform loses expectation consistency under direct inversion. This is not an implementation oversight: a direct inverse-transform estimator is universally expectation-consistent only when the inverse transform is affine, which cannot simultaneously provide bounded tail compression. Existing conditionally linear recovery methods restore expectation consistency, but still leave open which coordinate, inverse lookup, recovery base, and deployment monitor should be selected for sparse complex marginals. We propose \textbf{P}robability-\textbf{I}ntegral-\textbf{TranS}formed \textbf{Un}biased recovery (\textbf{PIT-SUN}), a deployable empirical marginal recovery framework. PIT-SUN uses one empirical marginal table to define a bounded normal-score coordinate, its inverse-quantile lookup, a variance-controlled recovery base, and drift monitoring, then applies multiplicative SUN recovery to estimate the original-space expectation instead of directly inverting transformed predictions. Experiments on synthetic distributions, public benchmarks, large-scale industrial datasets, and online deployment show robust improvements in point accuracy, calibration, and ranking quality with lightweight deployment overhead.

</details>


### [200] [RhyMix: A Lightweight Adaptive Multi-Rhythm Network for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08234)
*Sumit Satishrao Shevtekar,Chandresh Kumar Maurya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RhyMix是一种轻量级混合神经网络架构，采用并行双路径建模（循环路径+MSTCN-CA路径）与自适应门控机制，在12个真实世界时间序列数据集中的10个上达到SOTA，仅约40K参数且推理延迟<5ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测架构多采用单路径时序建模，存在各自局限：Transformer捕获长距离依赖但平滑局部变化，卷积捕获局部模式但感受野有限，线性模型高效但无法捕获非线性动力学。真实世界时序数据同时包含短期波动、周期性季节循环、长期趋势和不规则突变等多种复杂动态模式，需要能够同时处理这些模式的混合架构。

Method: 提出RhyMix混合架构，包含两条互补编码路径：(1) 循环路径(Cyclic Path)，通过可学习循环嵌入引入显式季节性归纳偏置，捕获可预测的节奏模式；(2) 轻量级多尺度时序卷积网络与通道注意力路径(MSTCN-CA Path)，采用多尺度深度可分离膨胀卷积捕获不同感受野下的时序依赖。关键创新为多层自适应门控：路径门(path gate)为每个样本和通道动态组合四个专用预测头（直接、趋势-季节分解、局部卷积、周期融合），混合门(hybrid gate)根据输入特征自适应平衡两条路径的输出。

Result: 在12个真实世界数据集的长期预测基准测试中，RhyMix在10个数据集上达到SOTA性能。模型仅约40K参数，具有线性复杂度，推理延迟<5ms，适合资源受限的边缘设备和实时部署。

Conclusion: RhyMix通过并行双路径设计与多层自适应门控机制，有效解决了单一建模范式无法同时捕获多种时序动态模式的问题，在保持轻量化和低延迟的同时实现了优异的预测性能，验证了混合架构在时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world time series exhibit complex dynamics characterized by multiple simultaneous temporal patterns: short-term fluctuations, periodic seasonal cycles, long-term trends, and irregular abrupt changes. However, many existing forecasting architectures rely on single-path temporal modeling--transformers capture long-range dependencies but smooth local variations, convolutions capture local patterns but have limited receptive fields, and linear models are efficient but cannot capture nonlinear dynamics. To address this, we introduce RhyMix (RHYthm MIXture), a hybrid neural architecture designed around a parallel dual-path modeling paradigm with adaptive gating mechanisms. RhyMix integrates two complementary encoding branches: (i) a Cyclic Path that incorporates explicit seasonal inductive bias through learnable cyclic embeddings, capturing predictable rhythmic patterns; and (ii) a lightweight Multi-Scale Temporal Convolutional Network with Channel Attention Path that employs multi-scale depthwise dilated convolutions to capture temporal dependencies across different receptive fields. A key innovation is the use of adaptive gating at multiple levels: a path gate dynamically combines four specialized forecasting heads (Direct, Trend-Seasonal Decomposition, Local Convolution, and Periodic Fusion) per sample and channel, while a hybrid gate adaptively balances the Cyclic and MSTCN-CA Paths based on input characteristics. This design ensures the model adapts to specific temporal patterns while maintaining linear complexity in sequence length, channels, and prediction horizon. Across extensive benchmarks on 12 real-world datasets for long-term forecasting, RhyMix achieves state-of-the-art performance on 10 of 12 datasets. The model remains lightweight (~40K params) with linear complexity and low-latency inference (<5ms),suitable for resource-constrained edge devices and real-time deployment.

</details>


### [201] [Ensemble Diversity Optimization for Subjective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08493)
*Xia Cui,Ziyi Huang,N. R. Abeynayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO)，通过可微分的联合优化框架学习集成权重、有效基数和校准，使用符号化多样性正则化器在保留或抑制标注者分歧之间导航，在四个主观文本分类基准上显著改善概率校准（交叉熵降低40-78%），同时保持竞争性F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观性NLP任务常存在系统性的标注者分歧，传统模型倾向于坍缩这种不确定性，需要能够表示不确定性而非消除不确定性的模型。

Method: 提出EDO框架：通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛端到端学习集成组成和大小，引入符号化多样性正则化器（基于验证数据调节），结合软F1替代损失、类别加权交叉熵处理不平衡，以及可靠性加权多样性调控集成内变异性。

Result: 在ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement四个基准上，相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting、WEL，交叉熵降低40-78%，Brier分数更低，F1具有竞争力，且与标注者分布对齐更好。

Conclusion: 联合优化集成结构与符号化多样性正则化器为监督学习中建模人类主观性提供了一种高效、模型无关的方法。

Abstract: Subjective NLP tasks often exhibit systematic annotator disagreement, requiring models that represent uncertainty rather than collapse it. We introduce Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO), a prediction-space framework that jointly optimizes ensemble weights, effective cardinality, and calibration through a unified differentiable objective. EDO learns ensemble composition and size end-to-end via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation and incorporates a signed diversity regularizer, tuned on validation data, to steer optimization toward either preserving or suppressing disagreement. This regularization prevents ensemble collapse and enables controlled navigation of the utility-calibration trade-off. The framework integrates a soft F1 surrogate, class-weighted cross-entropy to address imbalance, and reliability-weighted diversity to regulate intra-ensemble variability. Experiments on four subjective text-classification benchmarks (ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement) show that EDO substantially improves probabilistic calibration, reducing cross-entropy (40-78% depending on baseline) and lowering Brier scores relative to Soft-CE, Soft-MD, Top-5 Voting, and WEL, while maintaining competitive F1 and better alignment with annotator distributions. These results demonstrate that jointly optimizing ensemble structure with a signed diversity regularizer provides an efficient, model-agnostic approach for modeling human subjectivity in supervised learning.

</details>


### [202] [Structure Learning on Clustered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08238)
*Ryan Thompson,Matt P. Wand,Veerabhadran Baladandayuthapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向聚类数据的DAG结构学习方法，通过扩展混合模型中的固定和随机效应框架，在估计全局结构的同时捕获局部聚类效应。该方法利用可微图耦合机制确保无环性，并提供了收敛保证的一阶优化算法与统计可辨识性理论。实验表明该方法能检测出被其他方法遗漏的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG结构学习方法假设总体完全同质，无法应用于存在聚类特异性变异的聚类数据（如患者特异性效应），亟需一种能同时估计全局结构并处理局部聚类效应的方法。

Method: 将经典混合模型中的固定效应与随机效应框架扩展到结构学习场景，提出可微图耦合机制确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环性；设计一阶优化算法支持跨聚类的批量更新，并给出统计可辨识性证明与渐近一致性理论。

Result: 在真实与合成数据实验中，该方法能够检测到其他对比方法遗漏的关键依赖关系，验证了其在聚类场景下结构学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为异质聚类数据的因果发现提供了兼具理论保证与计算效率的新范式，拓展了DAG结构学习在生物医学等存在群体异质性领域的应用边界。

Abstract: Recent algorithmic advances have made directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning scalable for causal discovery. Yet, the currently available techniques assume a completely homogeneous population, precluding their application to clustered data where cluster-specific variations (e.g., patient-specific effects) are common. We address this issue by introducing a new approach that estimates a global structure while accounting for local cluster-level effects. The key idea is to extend the fixed- and random-effects framework of classical mixed models to the structure learning setting. Towards this end, we present a differentiable graph coupling mechanism that guarantees the union of the fixed- and random-effects graphs remains acyclic. Computationally, we provide a provably convergent first-order method and leverage efficient batched updates across clusters. Statistically, we establish identifiability of the model and show that our approach recovers the true structure asymptotically. In experiments on real and synthetic data, our proposal detects dependencies missed by alternative estimators, underscoring its value for structure learning in clustered settings.

</details>


### [203] [An interpretable Good--Turing restart criterion for k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08243)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GTRC准则，利用Good-Turing估计和置信度边界自适应决定k-means++重启次数，在36个数据集上达到与固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数会根据数据集难度自适应变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-means++算法固定重启次数的方式既浪费计算资源（简单数据集）又可能不足（困难数据集），且缺乏原则性的选择依据，影响实验结果的可比较性。

Method: GTRC结合三种技术：Good-Turing估计（预测新结果出现概率）、一个经过证明的无条件边界、以及一个基于置信度的边界，当进一步重启改善当前结果的概率低于用户指定容差ε时停止。

Result: 在36个数据集上，GTRC达到了与精心选择的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数随数据集难度自适应变化，由数据依赖的信号驱动而非固定规则。

Conclusion: GTRC为k-means++重启次数提供了一种有原则、可报告的替代方案，取代了预先固定重启次数的做法。

Abstract: The k-means++ algorithm is commonly restarted multiple times to avoid poor local optima, yet the number of restarts is almost always chosen arbitrarily and applied uniformly regardless of data set difficulty. This undermines any comparison relying on such a choice and wastes computation on easy data sets while potentially under-serving hard ones. We introduce GTRC, a restart criterion combining a Good-Turing estimate, a proven unconditional bound, and a confidence-based bound on the probability that a further restart would improve on the current result, stopping once this probability falls below a user-specified tolerance $\varepsilon$. Across 36 data sets, GTRC reached clustering quality competitive with well-chosen fixed restart counts, while the number of restarts used varied considerably and appropriately with data set difficulty, governed by an interpretable, data-dependent signal rather than a fixed rule. GTRC offers a principled and reportable alternative to fixing the number of $k$-means++ restarts in advance. Software:https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion.

</details>


### [204] [CASL-VAE: Learning Structured Latent Variables from Unpaired Data for Semi-supervised Clustering and Paired Sample Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08254)
*Sai Spandana Chintapalli,Pratik Chaudhari,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CASL-VAE是一种深度对比潜变量模型，能从非配对数据中学习结构化的潜在生成因子，将变异性分解为跨群体共享的连续公共因子和表示目标特异性异质性的分层显著因子（离散亚型+连续亚型内变异），在阿尔茨海默病数据上验证了其改进的亚型恢复和配对样本生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理非配对数据且存在异质性目标变异时，难以分离目标特异性的多种变异模式，亟需一种能在无配对数据条件下建模目标群体相对于参考群体多重变异模式的方法。

Method: 提出CASL-VAE，一种对比式深度潜变量模型，将变异分解为连续公共因子（跨群体共享）和分层显著因子（离散亚型+连续亚型内变异），利用变分推断在非配对数据上近似优化参考域和目标域的联合似然，实现配对样本生成和跨域分析。

Result: 在半合成神经影像数据上，CASL-VAE在亚型恢复和配对样本生成方面优于基线聚类方法和生成模型；在真实阿尔茨海默病数据上，揭示了具有生物学可解释性的异质性。

Conclusion: CASL-VAE提供了一种基于原理的非配对数据联合似然优化框架，能够有效分离多模态目标特异性变异，在临床和科学问题中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Quantifying variability in a target population relative to a reference population is central to many scientific and clinical problems (e.g., diseased vs. healthy). Yet, without paired data and in the presence of heterogeneous target variation, existing methods struggle to separate multiple modes of target-specific variation. We propose \textit{CASL-VAE}, a deep contrastive latent variable model that learns structured latent generative factors from unpaired data. CASL-VAE factorizes variation into continuous common latent factors shared across populations and hierarchical salient latent factors that model target-specific heterogeneity as discrete subtypes and continuous within-subtype variation. Using variational inference, we show how approximate joint likelihood optimization over reference and target domains can be performed using unpaired data, providing a principled basis for paired-sample generation and cross-domain analysis. We validate CASL-VAE on semi-synthetic neuroimaging data, demonstrating improved subtype recovery and paired-sample generation compared to baseline clustering and generative models. We also validate its ability to reveal biologically plausible heterogeneity in Alzheimer's disease.

</details>


### [205] [Classifier Chain-based Pathological Test Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08299)
*Abu Rafe Md Jamil,Nayan Malakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Classifier Chain技术的病理检验推荐系统，通过患者症状在医生问诊前推荐检验项目。实验表明Logistic回归+CC达到98.83%准确率，集成模型F1为0.89，并利用SHAP实现可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诊断过程中检验推荐延迟且依赖医生主观判断，影响诊疗效率和质量。本研究旨在通过症状驱动的自动检验推荐系统加速诊断流程，减少人为偏差。

Method: 将检验推荐建模为多标签分类问题，采用Classifier Chain技术捕捉检验项目间的依赖关系。收集SOUTHERN.IML病理数据并构建专家标注数据集，对比逻辑回归、决策树、随机森林等算法，使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: 逻辑回归+CC模型准确率最高达98.83%；多数投票集成模型在精确率0.93、召回率0.85、F1-score 0.89上表现最佳平衡。SHAP解释结果与医学知识一致。

Conclusion: Classifier Chain能有效提升传统算法在诊断过程中的效率，提供准确的检验推荐，且模型推理透明可信，可辅助医生在关键场景下做出合理决策。

Abstract: Accurate and timely diagnoses are essential for quality patient care. However, delayed recommendation of diagnostic tests and physicians' subjective interpretations can hinder effective care. This study introduces a pathological test recommendation system that speeds up the test selection process using patient symptoms before physician consultation. The recommendation task is framed as a multi-label classification problem utilising the Classifier Chain (CC) technique to consider dependencies between tests. We collected data from the SOUTHERN.IML pathology and then created a custom dataset with the help of the expertise. Multiple machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were applied to compare models and identify the best fit for our study context. The Logistic Regression with CC model had the highest overall accuracy at 98.83%, while the Majority Voting ensemble model provided the best balance with a precision of 0.93, recall of 0.85, and F1-score of 0.89. To ensure transparency of the models and clinical interpretability, we used Explainable AI (XAI) techniques utilising SHAP (SHapley Additive Explanations), which identifies how each symptom is contributing to a test recommendation. The diagnostic reasoning revealed by the model was consistent with established medical knowledge of symptoms for the recommended tests, which further adds confidence to the model's reliability for diagnostic purposes. The reasoning could help physicians make logical decisions in critical scenarios. Overall, our findings suggest that CC can improve the efficiency of the traditional algorithms in diagnostic process providing accurate test recommendations.

</details>


### [206] [Learning $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under Locally Sampleable Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08303)
*Weiming Feng,Xiongxin Yang,Yixiao Yu,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在具有高效局部采样器的图模型下学习AC^0的拟多项式时间算法，通过模拟和截断经典Glauber动力学建立新的低度近似，绕过了先前工作中对多项式增长的要求，并适用于任意有界度图上的双自旋系统（如硬核模型和Ising模型），在接近采样阈值的区域有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linial等人提出了在均匀分布下学习AC^0的拟多项式时间算法，但将其推广到更广泛的相关分布类（如图模型）一直是一个长期挑战。最近的工作虽然将保证扩展到了具有强空间混合和多项式增长的Gibbs分布，但仍受限于多项式增长条件。

Method: 通过模拟并适当截断经典的Glauber动力学，建立了Gibbs分布的新型低度近似，从而绕过了多项式增长要求。

Result: 提出了一个在具有高效局部采样器的图模型下学习AC^0的拟多项式时间学习器，适用于任意有界度图上的双自旋系统（硬核模型和Ising模型），在接近各自采样阈值的区域有效。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓展了AC^0在相关分布下的可学习性边界，通过图模型框架将学习保证推广到了更一般的设置，为计算学习理论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The problem of learning constant-depth circuits holds profound implications for computational learning theory. In a seminal result, by introducing the low-degree algorithm, Linial, Mansour, and Nisan (J. ACM 1993) presented a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under the uniform distribution. However, obtaining comparable learning guarantees for broader classes of correlated distributions has remained a longstanding challenge. Recently, Chandrasekaran, Gaitonde, Moitra, and Vasilyan (arXiv 2026) extended these guarantees to Gibbs distributions on bounded-degree graphical models with both strong spatial mixing and polynomial growth.
  In this paper, we give a quasipolynomial-time learner for $\mathsf{AC}^0$ under graphical models that admit efficient local samplers, circumventing the polynomial-growth requirement in prior work. The key ingredient is a new low-degree approximation for Gibbs distributions, established by simulating and suitably truncating the classical Glauber dynamics. As applications, this framework yields learners for two-spin systems, including the hard-core model and Ising model, on arbitrary bounded-degree graphs, in regimes approaching their respective sampling thresholds.

</details>


### [207] [Write-Protected Discrete Bottlenecks for Language-Grounded World Models: A Structural Limitation and Sufficient Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08312)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言梯度直接注入Gumbel-Softmax离散符号瓶颈会导致结构性的符号坍塌问题（2.2/64个有效符号），并提出了一个三层次解决方案：切断梯度、无梯度语义通道（记忆表）、DP-Means聚类解决符号冲突，在32个种子中实现零符号坍塌，语义绑定准确率达79-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑当前主流范式（端到端将LLM/VLM特征注入机器人世界模型）中"语言梯度可以直接塑造物理符号表征"这一隐含假设是否安全，并发现该假设存在结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出三层次约束：（1）z.detach()切断梯度链；（2）引入无梯度的语义通道——零参数零梯度的记忆表（Dict[symbol->Counter[label]]），用共现计数替代梯度绑定的语义学习；（3）采用DP-Means流式聚类处理符号冲突，自动分裂子聚类。

Result: 在74次独立实验中，32个种子全部实现零符号坍塌；语义绑定准确率97.2%（vs 无第三层时的22.2%）；跨三种编码器架构（CNN、V-JEPA 300M、CLIP ViT-L）、两种环境、三种纹理条件下达到79-100%的语义绑定率；训练参数少于2M，无需微调LLM。

Conclusion: 在GumbelBottleneck系列离散瓶颈中，语言梯度和符号多样性之间存在结构性矛盾（而非优化问题），所提三层次约束足以解决这一根本性失败。

Abstract: How should language interface with a world model's discrete symbol system? The dominant paradigm -- end-to-end injection of LLM/VLM features into robot world models (RT-2, Octo, PaLM-E) -- implicitly assumes that language gradients can directly shape physical symbol representations. We ask whether this assumption is safe, find that it is not, and characterize the minimal architectural constraint that prevents the failure. Any language gradient entering a Gumbel-softmax-based discrete symbol bottleneck forces a structural trade-off: the vanilla estimator collapses to 2.2/64 symbols (4/5 seeds), while five anti-collapse strategies maintain diversity but fail to learn semantic labels (all <= 9.2% accuracy). No tested GumbelBottleneck variant achieves both objectives simultaneously. Within this family of discrete bottlenecks, the failure is structural rather than a matter of optimization. We characterize a sufficient set of three constraints that prevent the failure: (1) cut the gradient chain (z.detach()), preventing language signals from reaching the symbol bottleneck; (2) provide a gradient-free semantic channel -- a non-parametric Memory Table (Dict[symbol -> Counter[label]], zero parameters, zero gradients) where co-occurrence counting replaces gradient-based binding; (3) handle symbol collisions via DP-Means streaming clustering for automatic sub-cluster splitting. All three layers together achieve 97.2% grounding accuracy vs. 22.2% without Layer 3. Across two experiments spanning 74 independent runs, we demonstrate zero symbol collapse in all 32 seeds, with the blackboard achieving 79-100% semantic binding across three encoder architectures (CNN, V-JEPA 300M, CLIP ViT-L), two environments, and three texture conditions. The fix trains fewer than 2M parameters and requires no LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [208] [ArtMine: Discovering and Formalizing Artistic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08331)
*Kaustubh Kumar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Blessin Varkey,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtMine框架，能从碎片化历史证据中自动发现和形式化艺术创作过程，通过溯因推理构建可解释的创作步骤图谱，实现过程级而非仅成品级的艺术生产建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI仅建模成品分布，无法捕捉艺术创作中的迭代决策、材料操作和情境影响；艺术工作流仅被碎片化记录，缺乏计算形式化方法。

Method: 将多源异构历史证据整合为结构化知识库；基于皮尔士溯因推理代理推断证据支持的生产步骤；将步骤转化为组合图谱和渲染提示；通过自反思优化生成与参考作品间的偏差。

Result: 在多位艺术家和艺术运动的开放域历史资料上进行了初步概念验证案例研究，表明碎片化文献证据能支持连贯、可解释、可审计的艺术工作流表示。

Conclusion: 通过建模创作过程而非仅最终作品，推动面向过程的人机协同创造力系统发展，可支持艺术解读、创意教育、反思性协作和文化生产的计算研究。

Abstract: Understanding how artworks are created requires reasoning about the iterative decisions, material operations, and contextual influences that shape artistic production. While recent generative AI systems can synthesize artworks with high fidelity, they primarily model distributions over finished artifacts rather than the creative processes underlying their creation. In practice, artistic workflows are only partially documented through fragmented sources such as archival records, preparatory studies, correspondence, etc., making process-level understanding difficult to formalize computationally. In this work, we introduce ArtMine, a framework for discovering and formalizing artistic processes from heterogeneous historical evidence. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous artwork evidence into a structured repository, from which a Peircean abductive agent infers evidence-grounded production steps. These steps are converted into a compositional graph and rendering prompt, then optimized through self-reflection over deviations between the generated and reference artworks. We provide a preliminary proof-of-concept case study using open-domain historical sources across multiple artists and artistic movements, demonstrating that fragmented documentary evidence can support coherent, interpretable, and auditable representations of artistic workflows. By modeling creative processes rather than only final artifacts, our work moves toward process-centred human-AI co-creativity systems that can support artistic interpretation, creative education, reflective collaboration, and computational studies of cultural production.

</details>


### [209] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散模型遗忘问题，提出一种两阶段框架Mysys，通过自动合成流形邻近锚点实现稳定遗忘。理论证明缺乏流形邻近锚点会导致法向空间漂移降低遗忘性能，并引入交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近的高效代理。实验表明该方法在概念移除和保留效用上显著优于现有方法，且能轻松集成到现有遗忘方法中提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散遗忘方法存在两类问题：基于锚点的方法依赖人工语义选择，存在有偏遗忘风险；无锚点方法因潜在更新无约束导致遗忘不鲁棒。本文旨在解决这些不稳定遗忘问题。

Method: 在流形假设下理论形式化扩散遗忘不稳定问题，证明缺乏流形邻近锚点会导致法向空间漂移。提出两阶段框架Mysys自动合成流形邻近锚点，并设计交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近的高效替代优化目标。

Result: 目标概念移除提升31.04%（CLIP分数），非目标效用提升4.18%（CLIP分数）。集成到现有方法后，概念移除平均提升6.30%，效用平均提升6.65%。

Conclusion: Mysys实现了鲁棒且无偏的遗忘，可有效集成到多种现有扩散遗忘方法中提升性能。

Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

</details>


### [210] [Spectral Analysis of Dueling Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08340)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化的表格型Dueling Q-learning，建立了无正则化、无投影、常数步长递归下的收敛保证，通过切换线性系统表示和有限时间误差界，揭示了价值和优势更新对Q函数不同分量的增益差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Dueling Q-learning在实际中（如DQN）广泛应用，但其理论理解仍然有限。现有工作仅分析了正则化形式的表格型Dueling Q-learning，纯表格更新的收敛性尚未完全理解。

Method: 对确定性Dueling Q-learning导出精确的切换线性系统表示；对采样随机版本给出期望意义的有限时间误差界。

Result: 建立了无正则化、无投影、常数步长递归下的收敛保证；揭示了价值和优势更新分别对Q函数中动作公共分量（值函数）和动作差分分量（优势函数）施加不同增益的作用机制。

Conclusion: 该分析补充了Dueling Q-learning的理论理解，明确了算法中价值和优势两个分支在更新过程中的差异化角色。

Abstract: Q-learning is a fundamental algorithm in reinforcement learning (RL) for solving discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) when the transition kernel is unknown. The deep Q-network (DQN) extends Q-learning by using a deep neural network for Q-function approximation, which makes Q-learning applicable to more practical high-dimensional problems. Dueling Q-learning decomposes the Q-function into a value function and an advantage function and learns the two components jointly, which can improve learning efficiency. However, the theoretical understanding of dueling Q-learning is still limited. Recent work has initiated an analysis of tabular dueling Q-learning, but existing guarantees focus on a regularized formulation and leave the pure tabular update less completely understood. This paper strengthens that line of analysis by adding a direct interpretation of the centered tabular decomposition and by establishing convergence guarantees for the unregularized, unprojected constant step-size recursion. In particular, we derive an exact switching linear system representation for deterministic dueling Q-learning and a finite-time error bound in expectation for the sampled stochastic version. The analysis clarifies how the value and advantage updates act as different gains on the action-common (value function) and action-differential (advantage function) components of the Q-function.

</details>


### [211] [Certified Interventional Fidelity: Anytime-Valid, Adaptive Evaluation of Causal Claims in Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08349)
*Amir Asiaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出认证干预保真度（CIF），为可解释性研究中的干预评估提供统计框架，通过将报告量形式化为因果估计量并给出置信区间和任意有效置信序列，包括在自适应干预采样下的有界混合重要性加权，从而区分稳定的因果声明与有限采样及评估选择的伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机械可解释性研究在评估干预实验（如交换隐藏状态、激活修补、消融组件等）时，通常仅报告点估计，但评估过程可能在运行时被监控或针对可疑失败进行自适应调整，这使得难以判断报告的保真度或修补效应是稳定的因果声明还是有限采样和评估选择的结果。缺乏统计严谨性导致结论可信度存疑。

Method: CIF首先将报告的量形式化为因果估计量（causal estimand），即对某个有界分数在指定输入分布和指定干预分布上的期望。然后提供置信区间和任意有效置信序列（anytime-valid confidence sequences），包含在自适应干预采样下通过有界混合重要性加权（bounded mixture importance weighting）进行处理。具体实例化采用Hoeffding型序列和方差自适应投注序列（variance-adaptive betting sequences）。

Result: 在MNIST抽象和GPT-2 Small IOI电路上的实验表明，CIF能够认证高保真度声明，揭示看似方法差异但实际缺乏统计支持的情况，并使干预分布的敏感性显式化。方差自适应投注序列相比Hoeffding型序列将认证成本降低10-30倍。

Conclusion: CIF为干预式可解释性评估提供了必要的统计层，通过形式化因果估计量和提供有效置信序列，能够区分真实因果效应与采样噪声及评估偏差，为可解释性研究中的声明提供了统计保障。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often evaluates explanations by intervening on a model: swapping hidden states, patching activations, ablating components, or comparing a compressed model to the original one. These experiments are usually summarized by a point estimate, even though the evaluation may be monitored while it runs or adapted toward suspected failures. This makes it hard to tell whether a reported fidelity or patching effect is a stable causal claim or a consequence of finite sampling and evaluation choices. We introduce Certified Interventional Fidelity (CIF), a statistical layer for interventional interpretability evaluations. CIF first writes the quantity being reported as a causal estimand: an expectation of a bounded score over a stated input distribution and a stated intervention distribution. It then provides confidence intervals and anytime-valid confidence sequences for this estimand, including under adaptive intervention sampling via bounded mixture importance weighting. We instantiate CIF with Hoeffding-style sequences and variance-adaptive betting sequences, the latter reducing certification cost by 10-30x in our experiments. On MNIST abstractions and GPT-2 Small IOI circuits, CIF certifies high-fidelity claims, shows when apparent method differences are not statistically supported, and makes sensitivity to the intervention distribution explicit.

</details>


### [212] [Self-Adaptive Anomaly Detection with Reinforcement Learning and Human Feedback in Connected Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08373)
*Matthias Weiß,Athreya Hosahalli Prakash,Maurice Artelt,Falk Dettinger,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向自主网联车辆系统的在线异常检测框架，结合深度Q网络自适应选择检测器、统计漂移检测集成机制和人机协同重训练策略，在微服务拓扑上实现持续监控与自适应更新，避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网联车辆作为自主信息物理系统，运行中需持续监控异常，但系统本身会因OTA更新、配置变更、工作负载变化而演化，导致静态诊断方法性能静默退化。现有方法未能将模型自适应与操作员知识整合为统一的监督回路。

Method: 1) 基于自注意力机制的分裂深度Q网络从候选池中为每个微服务选择最合适的检测器，利用服务间依赖关系；2) 三个统计漂移检测器组成的集成表决机制，三者一致才触发报警，优先保证精确率；3) 人在环重训练机制，采用待转移缓冲区和60/40优先级重放策略，融合专家知识同时保留历史分布学习结果。

Result: 在自动代客泊车应用的七微服务测试平台上，注意力增强智能体的F1分数达0.69，而任何单一检测器统一应用最高仅0.11。软件更新引起概念漂移后F1降至0.52，经操作员触发重训练后在新分布上恢复至0.65，同时旧分布保持0.69，具备持续适应且无灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 提出的在线异常检测框架通过检测器自适应选择、集成漂移检测和人机协同重训练三个协同机制，有效解决了自主CPS在演化过程中静态方法性能退化的问题，实现了持续适应且不遗忘已学知识。

Abstract: Connected vehicles are autonomous cyber-physical systems whose behavior must be continuously monitored during operation to detect deviations from normal operation before they propagate into failures. Such evaluation is challenging because the systems themselves evolve: over-the-air updates, configuration changes, and shifting workloads alter the definition of normal behavior, causing static diagnostic methods to degrade silently over time. Existing approaches typically address either automated model adaptation or operator integration in isolation, rather than as a single coordinated supervisory loop.
  This paper presents an online anomaly detection framework for autonomous CPS that integrates three coordinated mechanisms. A factorized deep Q-network with self-attention selects the most suitable detector from a candidate pool for each monitored service, exploiting inter-service dependencies in the microservice topology. An ensemble of three statistical drift detectors monitors the input distribution and raises an alarm only when all three concur, prioritizing precision over recall. A human-in-the-loop retraining mechanism, built around a pending transition buffer and a 60/40 prioritized replay strategy, allows the operator to incorporate expert knowledge while preserving the system's learned response to prior data distributions.
  The framework is evaluated on a connected-vehicle testbed running an automated valet parking application across seven backend microservices. The attention-augmented agent achieves an F1 score of 0.69, compared to at most 0.11 for any single detector applied uniformly. Following a real software update that induces measurable concept drift, F1 drops to 0.52; after operator-triggered retraining, performance recovers to 0.65 on the new distribution while remaining at 0.69 on the prior one, demonstrating sustained adaptation without catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [213] [Eigenvalue Calibration for Semantic Embeddings of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08377)
*Sebastian G. Gruber,Nassim Walha,Francis Bach,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过温度缩放校准语义嵌入特征值的新框架，将LLM与语义嵌入解释为密度矩阵预测器，建立了熵-风险等价性，证明了温度缩放特征值在校准中的最优性，并在实验中验证了LLM的系统性过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类概率的校准结果无法直接迁移到语义嵌入的特征值上，而特征值已成为最先进不确定性量化方法的关键工具，现有研究缺乏针对特征值的校准框架。

Method: 将LLM及其生成答案的语义嵌入解释为密度矩阵预测器，提出通过温度缩放校准特征值的方法；建立熵-风险等价性，推导特征值专属的中心校准不等式，证明温度缩放特征值在最小化适当评分风险时优化校准。

Result: 在多种真实场景下的实验表明，当前LLM存在系统性过度自信，同时验证了理论发现的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了语义嵌入不确定性量化的理论基础与实践应用，为LLM的可靠部署提供了新的校准工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs), and eigenvalues of semantic embeddings have recently emerged as a key tool in state-of-the-art methods. However, conventional calibration results developed for classification probabilities cannot be directly transferred to eigenvalues. We address this gap by proposing a novel framework for calibrating the eigenvalues of semantic embeddings. We interpret LLMs combined with semantic embeddings of their generated answers as density matrix predictors, and we propose a novel approach to calibrate density matrix predictors by applying temperature scaling to their eigenvalues. We establish entropy-risk equivalence under calibration, derive a central calibration inequality specific to eigenvalues, and prove that temperature-scaled eigenvalues optimize calibration when minimizing proper score risks. Experiments on a variety of real-world settings show that current LLMs are systematically overconfident, and validate our theoretical findings. Together, these results advance the foundations and practice of uncertainty quantification for semantic embeddings.

</details>


### [214] [Dynamics of Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Near a Manifold of Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08380)
*Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了梯度下降大学习率理论，将过参数化最小二乘从单标量输出推广到向量输出，并将孤立平坦最小值推广到平坦最小值流形，应用于深度矩阵分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典梯度下降理论要求步长小于锐度倒数的两倍，但深度神经网络训练中常违反此条件。近期工作虽在过参数化最小二乘（单标量输出）中弥合了这一差距，但局限于孤立平坦最小值。本文旨在将其推广到更实际的向量输出和平坦最小值流形场景。

Method: (1) 将正常形和三个收敛定理从单标量输出推广到向量输出（含任意多观测值）；(2) 将分析从孤立平坦最小值邻域推广到平坦最小值流形邻域；(3) 通过求解奇异偏微分方程的新方法克服技术挑战；(4) 应用于深度矩阵分解，刻画其平坦最小值集合的几何结构。

Result: (1) 建立了向量输出场景下的正常形和三个收敛定理；(2) 证明了平坦最小值流形存在的必要性（如矩阵分解）；(3) 对于深度矩阵分解，证明了平坦最小值集构成球面乘积上的纤维丛，且锐度沿该流形是Morse-Bott的。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了大步长梯度下降理论的应用范围，为理解深度学习中违反经典步长条件的现象提供了更通用的理论框架，且提出的奇异PDE求解方法可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: An important quantity in the theory of gradient descent (GD) is the \emph{sharpness}, defined as the largest eigenvalue of the objective Hessian. Classical analyses typically require the step size to be uniformly smaller than twice the reciprocal of the sharpness, but this condition is frequently violated in the training of deep neural networks. Recent work bridges this gap in the setting of overparametrised least-squares with a \emph{single scalar output}, providing a normal form for large-step GD in a neighbourhood of an \emph{isolated} flat minimum and establishing three corresponding convergence results. In this paper, we extend this theory in two directions: (1) to overparametrised least-squares with \emph{vector-valued outputs} (including regression with arbitrarily many observations), and (2) to a neighbourhood of a \emph{manifold} of flat minima (which we show is essential for applications such as matrix factorisation). We generalise both the normal form and all three convergence theorems of \cite{macdonaldeos} to this broader setting, overcoming several technical challenges, including the solution of a singular partial differential equation via a novel method that may be of independent interest. We further show that our framework applies to deep matrix factorisation under mild assumptions, yielding several new structural results. In particular, we prove that the set of flat minima forms a fibre bundle over a product of spheres, and that the sharpness is Morse-Bott along this manifold.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond Backpropagation: Monte Carlo Method Can Train Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08406)
*Hong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——随机变异一个参数，若损失降低则保留，否则重试——可以在单个GPU上有效训练深度网络。该方法无需批归一化或残差连接等常见技术，支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重、非常规激活函数（如高斯函数），并在超过20层的深度网络、单隐藏层宽网络（最多16384个神经元）以及简单Transformer架构（图像分类和字符级语言建模）上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖梯度带来了固有的梯度消失和梯度爆炸问题，长期以来，寻找无梯度方法一直是人工智能领域的目标。

Method: 采用最简单的蒙特卡洛算法：随机变异网络中一个参数，如果损失下降则保留该变异，否则回退并重试。该方法在单个GPU上实现，无需批归一化、残差连接等常见技术。

Result: 成功训练了超过20层的深度网络、单隐藏层宽网络（高达16384个隐藏神经元），以及简单的Transformer架构，适用于图像分类（MNIST）和字符级语言建模（Tiny Shakespeare）任务。支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重和非常规激活函数（如高斯函数）。

Conclusion: 该简单无梯度方法可能为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供补充视角，也为构建受物理启发的深度学习系统提供了替代路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning training, but its reliance on gradients brings inherent troubles -- vanishing and exploding gradients. The pursuit of gradient-free methods has long been a goal in the field of artificial intelligence. This paper shows that indeed the simplest Monte Carlo algorithm implemented on a single GPU -- randomly mutate a parameter, keep it if the loss decreases, otherwise retry -- can practically train deep networks. This gradient-free method does not even need common techniques such as batch normalization or residual connections to directly train sufficiently deep networks. More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

</details>


### [216] [Predicting Male Fertility Using Machine Learning: A Semen Parameters Based Analysis with the VISEM Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08429)
*Shahnawaz Qureshi,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Muhammad Fozan,Emal Kawal,Syed Aziz Shah,Sattam Al-Anazi,Syed MuhammadZeeshan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用VISEM数据集和LazyPredict框架，测试了40多种机器学习算法，发现Nearest Centroid分类器在基于精液参数（浓度、活力、形态）分类男性生育状态（可育、亚不育、不育）时准确率达94.2%，表明机器学习可提供快速客观的精液质量评估，辅助临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是生殖健康中常见但诊断不足的问题，精液分析是临床评估的核心，但传统方法可能存在主观性和效率不足，因此需要更客观、准确的自动化分类方法。

Method: 采用VISEM数据集（85名参与者的精液样本），依据WHO标准分为可育、亚不育、不育三类。经过预处理和特征工程后，使用LazyPredict框架对40多种分类模型进行训练和评估，通过5折交叉验证和多类ROC-AUC分析验证模型鲁棒性。

Result: Nearest Centroid分类器以94.2%的准确率表现最佳，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型能够为精液质量提供快速、准确、客观的评估，有望支持男科和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策，并推动个性化治疗策略的制定。

Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

</details>


### [217] [MatBind: A Shared Embedding Space for Multimodal Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08470)
